HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000904/B001","root_000904/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_1cd7293782b7e5a390ed","text":"2. **Daylight as productive, public time.** Lexical evidence: ض ح و B001 \"forenoon daylight extending through the day\"; ض ح و B003 \"forenoon meal and early-day grazing\"; ف ل ح B003 \"tiller who splits the ground\" and B005 \"success with lasting good\" in 91:9; ز ك و B001 \"growth and increase\" in 91:9. Structural cue: early light leads, in the packet's middle, to cultivation/growth language around the soul. Assigned role: forenoon is an activator of work, feeding, and growth. Abductive move: I infer that moral purification is figured by productive daylight cultivation; the packet supplies forenoon/grazing plus tilling/growth branches. Reading change: \"her duha\" becomes an ecological and agricultural opening in which successful self-purification is like growth under usable light.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000904/B001","root_000904/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_37808a85fb879bee1dab","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:1:2\",\"root_000904\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Kuşluk, yalnızca parlak bir an değil, çalışma, beslenme ve büyümenin gerçekleşebildiği düzenli ve kullanışlı bir zaman aralığıdır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000818/B001","root_000904/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_44a6dd8440d73787dadd","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:1:1\",\"root_000818\",[\"B001\"]],[\"91:1:2\",\"root_000904\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Bu yoğun aydınlık, ileride ışığa rağmen kıvılcım üretemeyen başarısızlığı teşhis eden bir ölçüye dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000047/B002","root_000818/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_44e8aa1e4d64413e95f5","text":"3. **The sun as a potentially worshipped object recontained by the divine name.** Lexical evidence: ش م س B001 sun; ء ل ه B001 \"worship and the worshipped one, including the sun when named for being worshipped\"; ء ل ه B002 fixed Allah-name in 91:0 and 91:13. Structural cue: the sun appears after the basmalah and before a later \"messenger of Allah\" and \"she-camel of Allah.\" Assigned role: the sun's possible object-of-worship branch is activated only to be subordinated by the framing divine name. Abductive move: I infer that the oath's object is witness, not deity; the packet supplies the root branch where sun may be worshipped and the repeated Allah anchoring. Reading change: the ayah holds a polemical edge: the magnificent sun is not a rival god but a witnessed creature under Allah's name.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000818/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_54ad50c4cda59f117f84","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:1:1\",\"root_000818\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Güneşin önde, diğer göksel evrelerin ardından gelmesi, onu yerinde tutulamayan hareketli bir öncü gibi okumaya elverir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000186/B001","root_000818/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_5e8dedf29a785815df3d","text":"2. **An uncontained leader of the procession.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ش م س/B002` carries the dormant image of a restive being that refuses to settle or be ridden; 91:2 `ت ل و/B001` supplies bodily or sequential following. **Structural cue and role:** the sun leads, the moon follows, day manifests, and night overtakes, giving the otherwise nominal sun a mobile role. **Abductive move:** animating the celestial succession as restive motion is an exploratory inference; the weak link is that the fixed surface is the ordinary noun `الشمس`, not a restive derivative. **Reading change:** alongside the literal luminary, the sun reads as a leading force that cannot be held in place.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000904/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_7b3675a4d1a933dcbda9","text":"3. **An extended operating interval.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ض ح و/B001` is the forenoon's rising and extension; contextual `ب ن ي/B001` in 91:5 joins a structure and `ط ح و/B001` in 91:6 spreads a surface. **Structural cue and role:** duration sits inside a built-above/spread-below spatial frame, so `ضحاها` supplies the interval during which that world is exposed and usable. **Abductive move:** sky and earth are packet-given; assigning their construction as what enables the recurring interval is inferred. **Reading change:** `ضحاها` becomes organized duration within a system, not a momentary flash of light.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000818/B001","root_000904/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_7e42a91bad165a9464fb","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"91:1:1\",\"root_000818\",[\"B001\"]],[\"91:1:2\",\"root_000904\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Kuşluk aydınlığı, ilerideki açığa çıkarma ve gizleme karşıtlığını kuran kamusal bir görünürlük alanıdır; Structural cue: the first oath pairs the sun with its forenoon brightness, then the packet later contrasts manifestation with concealment. Assigned role: ضحى is the exposing surface on which hidden moral insertion can be judged. Abductive move: I supply the direction from physical brightness to epistemic exposure; the packet supplies daylight, unveiling, and concealment.
